	# [equipment-selection] (2026-08-13, checklist R35): the Comet had NO design in any ENG group, so the
	# AI auto-designed it and built it with 30+ factories from 1944.6 to the end of campaign
	# 02bd4445 - outside the frontier ranking, outside the WA_AI_EQUIPMENT_* resource gates,
	# and outside the emitted production_upgrade_desire_offset blocks, which is why
	# WA_AI_PRODUCTION_COUNTRY_ENG_TANKS.txt carried a lone +100 on the Cromwell and no
	# suppression at all. Note medium_tank_7 above already superseded itself on
	# `has_tech = eng_medium_tank_chassis_5` - the chain was written for a Comet design that
	# was never authored. Modules are the chassis' own `default_modules`, audited slot by
	# slot against `allowed_module_categories` (all 9 mountable, all 4 required slots filled).
	tank_eng_medium_chassis_5 = { #Comet
		# No supersession modifier on purpose. Every other design in this group is zeroed by
		# the next chassis tech in the SAME role (medium_tank_7 by eng_medium_tank_chassis_5,
		# i.e. by this design). The Comet has no successor medium chassis - what
		# eng_medium_tank_chassis_5 leads to is eng_modern_tank_chassis_1, a different role
		# owned by ENG_modern_tanks. Hooking the Comet to it would empty the medium role the
		# moment ENG researches modern tanks, which is exactly the production gap the
		# frontier exists to prevent.
		priority = {
			factor = 100
		}
		enable = {
			NOT = { WA_AI_EQUIPMENT_should_mount_weak_armor = yes }
			NOT = { WA_AI_EQUIPMENT_should_mount_low_ammo = yes }
			has_tech = eng_medium_tank_chassis_5
		}

		history = yes
		target_variant = {
			match_value = 3000
			type = tank_eng_medium_chassis_5
			modules = {
				main_armament_slot = eng_tank_semi_large_main_armament_1
				turret_type_slot = tank_eng_comet_turret_type_1
				engine_type_slot = tank_eng_large_engine_type_1
